# Invasion of Poland - M-R pact version

event = {
	id = 3183020
	random = no
	country = SOV
	
	decision = {
		war = { country = GER country = POL }
		NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = POL } }
		event = 2003025 #M-R pact
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			lost_national = { country = POL value = 15 }
			NOT = { control = { province = 485 data = POL } } #Warsaw
			event = { id = 2003036 days = 365 } 
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		NOT = { lost_national = { value = 5 } } #Might be fighting against Japan at the same time
		war = { country = GER country = POL }
		NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = POL } }
		event = 2003025 #M-R pact
		OR = {
			lost_national = { country = POL value = 15 }
			NOT = { control = { province = 485 data = POL } } #Warsaw
			event = { id = 2003036 days = 365 } 
		}
	}
	
	name = "Liberate Our Western Lands"
	desc = "Our current western border was drawn in the aftermath of the Polish-Soviet war which was fought 15 years earlier and ended inconlusively. It left much of our former territory under Polish rule, including many of our Ukrainian and Belorussian brothers. Even more importantly, it left the security of our country in a somewhat fragile state, with the Eastern polish border only 500km away from Moscow and even closer to Leningrad. If Germany were to take all of Poland, this would be an enormous security problem. While the pact we signed with Germany promises Eastern Poland to us, it is abundantly apparent that agreements reached with Hitler mean nothing unless they are backed up by force. For this reason alone, we must act, and act quickly."
	style = 2
	picture = "Poland_Invasion"
	
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 3
	deathdate = { day = 0 month = december year = 1945 }	

	action = {
		name = "The Liberation Campaign of the Red Army starts today"
		command = { type = dissent value = -1 }
		command = { type = war which = POL }
		command = { type = belligerence value = 5 }
		command = { type = setflag which = SOVPolAttack }
		command = { type = ai which = "nations\SOV_FIN.ai" }
		command = { type = ai which = "frontAIhyper.ai" }
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAICautious }
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAINormal }
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAIAggressive }
		command = { type = local_clrflag which = FrontAIVeryAggressive }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FrontAIHyper }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FrontAILock }
		
		#Allies have 30 days to decide how to react
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = POL country = ENG } } type = non_aggression which = ENG where = SOV when = 30 } 
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = POL country = VIC } } type = non_aggression which = VIC where = SOV when = 30 }
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = POL country = CAN } } type = non_aggression which = CAN where = SOV when = 30 }
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = POL country = AST } } type = non_aggression which = AST where = SOV when = 30 }
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = POL country = U02 } } type = non_aggression which = U02 where = SOV when = 30 }
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = POL country = NZL } } type = non_aggression which = NZL where = SOV when = 30 }
		command = { trigger = { event = 2003025 } type = access which = GER }
		
		command = { trigger = { ai = SIK } 	type = event 	which = 2400040 where = SIK when = 0 } #0 exp
		command = { trigger = { ai = SOV } 	type = event 	which = 2400047 where = SOV when = 0 } #70 exp
		command = { trigger = { ai = TAN } 	type = event 	which = 2400047 where = TAN when = 0 } #70 exp
		command = { trigger = { ai = MON } 	type = event 	which = 2400042 where = MON when = 0 } #20 exp
		command = { trigger = { puppet = { country = SIK country = SOV } }	type = military_control which = SOV where = SIK value = 1 }
		
		command = { 
			trigger = {
				#war = { country = ENG country = SOV }
				war = { country = ENG country = GER }
				war = { country = SOV country = POL }
				war = { country = GER country = POL }
			}
		type = event which = 2007019 where = ENG when = 0 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2007019
	random = no
	country = ENG
	save_date = yes
	
	#trigger = {
	#	war = { country = SOV }
	#	war = { country = GER }
	#	OR = {
	#		non_aggression = { country = ENG country = SOV } 
	#		AND = {
	#			war = { country = SOV country = POL }
	#			war = { country = GER country = POL }
	#		}
	#	}
	#}
	
	name = "Soviet Invasion of Poland"
	desc = "The news of the Molotov-Ribbentrop pact was met with utter shock and surprise by reporters worldwide, most being aware only of the British–French–Soviet negotiations that had taken place for months. Even fewer had guessed that in addition to stipulations of non-aggression, the treaty included a secret protocol dividing several eastern European countries between the parties. Not long after Germany attacked Poland, the Soviet troops moved in from the East and also engaged the Polish army. As allies of Poland and guarantors of Polish independence, we now have a difficult decision to make."
	style = 2
	picture = "Poland_Invasion"
	
	#date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	#offset = 15
	#deathdate = { day = 0 month = december year = 1945 }	

	action = {
		name = "We will deal with them later"
		ai_chance = 100
		command = { type = dissent value = 3 } #Poles and many others are upset. Are the British guarantees truly meaningful?
		command = { type = relation which = POL value = -50 }
		command = { type = relation which = EST value = -30 }
		command = { type = relation which = LAT value = -30 }
		command = { type = relation which = LIT value = -30 }
		command = { type = relation which = FIN value = -30 }
		command = { type = relation which = ROM value = -30 }
		command = { type = trigger	which = 20070210 }
		command = { type = peace which = SOV value = 1 }
	}
	action = {
		name = "Aggression is aggression. Our guarantees cannot discriminate."
		ai_chance = 0
		command = { type = dissent value = 2 } #Socialists and Communists are upset. The military is upset - Britain is up against an impossible challenge
		command = { type = sleepevent which = 20070210 }
		#The non-aggression will expire on its own. The USSR is now in a full-scale war with the Allies. German AI will not do Barbarossa.
	}
}

event = {
	id = 20070210
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	all = yes
	
	name = "Soviet Invasion of Poland"
	desc = "Not long after Germany attacked Poland, the Soviet troops moved in from the East and also engaged the Polish army. As allies of Poland and guarantors of Polish independence, the UK had a difficult decision to make. When Winston Churchill gave a speech in which he effectively justified the Soviet move, calling it a necessary preacution against Nazi menace, it became clear that was against the Soviet union was not yet in the cards..."
	style = 2
	picture = "Poland_Invasion"
	
	action = {
		name = "We will deal with them later"
		trigger = {
			NOT = { country = ENG country = POL }
			alliance = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = SOV }
		}
		command = { type = peace which = SOV value = 1 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2007034
	random = no
	country = SOV
	
	name = "EVT_2650_NAME" 
	desc = "As it turns out, Ribbentrop's mission in Moscow was just a stalling tactic rather than a genuine attempt to reach an agreement. Instead, Germany called our bluff and attacked Poland without our blessing. No matter what we do now, it clear that the clash against Germany is both inevitable and imminent. If we let them take Poland, we will be next, there is no question about it. The cursed British have gotten exactly what they wanted - Germany and USSR will fight each other while they watch from across the channel and make money. Even so, we have no choice in the matter any more, we urgently need their help. We can sign a formal treaty promising to respect Poland's territorial integrity, then we will be allowed to enter Poland and save the Polish army from destruction. Perhaps we can still subjugate Poland later..."
	style = 2
	picture = "Poland_Invasion"
	
	trigger = { 
		NOT = {
			event = 2003025
			non_aggression = { country = GER country = SOV }
		}
		#atwar = no
		war = { country = GER country = POL }
	}
	
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 5
	deathdate = { day = 28 month = january year = 1943 }

	action = {
		name = "Sign the treaty, ask for permission"
		ai_chance = 100
		#command = { type = war which = GER }
		command = { type = dissent value = 2 }
		command = { type = event which = 3183021 where = ENG when = 1 }
	}
	action = {
		name = "We need nobody's permission"
		ai_chance = 0
		command = { type = war which = GER }
		command = { type = war which = POL }
		command = { type = belligerence value = 5 }
		command = { type = dissent value = -1 }
	}
	action = {
		name = "Just grab as much of Poland as we can and hope for the best"
		ai_chance = 0
		command = { type = war which = POL }
		command = { type = belligerence value = 5 }
		command = { type = end_trades which = GER where = SOV }
		command = { type = embargo which = SOV where = GER value = 1 }
		command = { type = dissent value = 10 }
	}
	action = {
		name = "Just hope for the best..."
		ai_chance = 0
		command = { type = end_trades which = GER where = SOV }
		command = { type = embargo which = SOV where = GER value = 1 }
		command = { type = dissent value = 15 }
	}
}

Thus our main goal has already been achieved - Germany and USSR will fight each other while we will intervene only as much as we deem necessary. Even so, if we don't give the Soviets permission to enter Poland even under these circumstances, they will probably attack anyway. }

#########################################################################
#  Operation Icebreaker
#########################################################################
event = {
	id = 3183024
	random = no
	country = SOV
	
	name = "Operation Icebreaker" 
	desc = "With Poland out of the way, we turn our attention to the real threat and our main prize - Nazi Germany. It is our duty and our destiny to destroy the Fascist beast in the heart of Europe. And now we has a brief window of opportunity to do just that. Germany is weak and occupied by bloody battles in other theatres. Its stockpiles of oil and raw materials are quickly running dry. The time to strike is now."
	style = 2
	picture = "Poland_Invasion"
	
	decision = {
		NOT = { war = { country = GER }	}
		OR = {
			control = { province = 485 data = -3 } # Warzaw
			control = { province = 485 data = GER }
			alliance = { country = GER country = POL }
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		belligerence = { country = GER value = 80 }
	}
	trigger = { 
		NOT = { 
			war = { country = GER }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = FIN }
			war = { country = SWE }
			war = { country = ROM }
		}
		OR = {
			control = { province = 485 data = -3 } # Warzaw
			control = { province = 485 data = GER }
			alliance = { country = GER country = POL }
		}
		belligerence = { country = GER value = 80 }
		
		control = { province = 123 data = SOV } #Terijoki (Finland defeated OR gave in)
	
		war = { country = GER country = ENG } #Germany distracted
		region = { region = "England" country = ENG value = 30 } #England fighting
		OR = {
			alliance = { country = ENG country = USA }
			AND = {
				war = { country = GER country = VIC }
				NOT = { control = { province = 56 data = GER } }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			#Germany is invaded by the Allies
			lost_national = { country = GER value = 10 }
			#Germany is invading England
			AND = {
				flag = ENG_invaded
				#random = 30
			}
			# Finished reogranization of the Red Army (Nov 1942) and ready to strike
			AND = {
				event = { id = 2007030 days = 30 } 
				OR = {
					NOT = { event = 2003025 } #M-R pact
					event = { id = 2003025 days = 730 } # 2 years
				}
				month = 3
				NOT = { month = 9 }	
				random = 40
			}
			#Germany is attacking France but has almost no defenses on the border. Unfortunately, cannot count allied troops, counting only Germans
			AND = {
				NOT = { ai = GER }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = GER province = 485 type = land size = 20 area = 2 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = GER province = 510 type = land size = 10 area = 1 } }
				war = { country = GER country = VIC }
				OR = {
					war = { country = GER country = BEL }
					alliance = { country = GER country = BEL }
					control = { province = 51 data = GER }
				}
			}
		}
		#USSR has plenty of troops on the border
		garrison = { country = SOV province = 503 type = land size = 50 area = 2 }
		
		#Under normal circumstances, USSR will attack in early 1943
		#Will also attack if Germany does sealion, but will wait for the US to join first.
	}
	
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 28 month = january year = 1943 }

	action = {
		name = "evt_4700a"
		#Leaving net +3 dissent if there was a non-aggression pact. Otherwise, no dissent.
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = GER } } type = local_setflag which = NAPDissent }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = end_non_aggression which = SOV where = GER } #will give 5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = dissent value = -2 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = belligerence which = SOV	value = 20  }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = belligerence which = GER	value = -20 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = local_clrflag which = NAPDissent }
		
		#finland may also have NAP, removing it in full
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = FIN } } type = local_setflag which = NAPDissent }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = end_non_aggression which = SOV where = FIN } #will give 5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = dissent value = -5 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = belligerence which = SOV	value = 5  }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = belligerence which = FIN	value = -5 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = local_clrflag which = NAPDissent }
		
		command = { type = war which = GER } 
	}
}

#  Manchurian Operation

event = {
	id = 3183025
	random = no
	country = SOV
	
	name = "Manchurian Operation" 
	desc = "With Japan distracted and preoccupied with threats elsewhere, now is a good time to invade Manchuria and crush the Japanese threat once and for all."
	style = 2
	picture = "Marco_Polo_bridge"
	
	decision = {
		NOT = { war = { country = JAP }	}
		OR = {
			control = { province = 2241 data = -3  } # Warzaw
			control = { province = 2241 data = JAP }
			alliance = { country = JAP country = MAN }
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		belligerence = { country = JAP value = 80 }
	}
	trigger = { 
		NOT = { 
			#war = { country = GER }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = ENG }
			#war = { country = FIN }
			#war = { country = SWE }
			#war = { country = ROM }
		}
		OR = {
			control = { province = 2241 data = -3  } # Warzaw
			control = { province = 2241 data = JAP }
			alliance = { country = JAP country = MAN }
		}
		belligerence = { country = JAP value = 80 }
		#control = { province = 123 data = SOV } #Terijoki (Finland defeated OR gave in)
	
		NOT = { lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 10 } }
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
			# Finished reogranization of the Red Army (Nov 1942) and ready to strike
			event = { id = 2007030 days = 30 } 
		}
		#Japan has almost no defenses on the border. Unfortunately, cannot count allied troops, counting only Japanese
		NOT = { ai = JAP }
		NOT = { garrison = { country = JAP province = 1379 type = land size = 12 area = 3 } } #Manchuria
		NOT = { garrison = { country = JAP province = 1199 type = land size = 10 area = 2 } } #Korea
		war = { country = JAP country = ENG } #Japan distracted
		war = { country = JAP country = USA }
		war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
		#USSR has plenty of troops on the border
		garrison = { country = SOV province = 1525 type = land size = 15 area = 2 }
	}
	
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 28 month = january year = 1943 }

	action = {
		name = "evt_4700a"
		#Leaving net +3 dissent if there was a non-aggression pact. Otherwise, no dissent.
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = JAP } } type = local_setflag which = NAPDissent }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = end_non_aggression which = SOV where = JAP } #will give 5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = dissent value = -3 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = belligerence which = SOV	value = 20  }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = belligerence which = JAP	value = -20 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = local_clrflag which = NAPDissent }
		
		#manchuria may also have NAP, removing it in full
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = MAN } } type = local_setflag which = NAPDissent }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = end_non_aggression which = SOV where = MAN } #will give 5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = dissent value = -5 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = belligerence which = SOV	value = 5  }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = belligerence which = MAN	value = -5 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = NAPDissent } type = local_clrflag which = NAPDissent }
		
		command = { type = war which = JAP } 
	}
}

event = {
	id = 3183005
	random = no
	country = SOV
	save_date = yes
	
	decision = {

event = {
	id = 3183037
	random = no
	country = SOV
	
	decision = {
		event = 20070260 #Demands to Finland
		NOT = { alliance = { country = FIN country = GER } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = FIN country = ENG } }
		NOT = { event = 2030022 } #defeat in cont. war
		NOT = { war = { country = FIN } }
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		#NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = FIN } }
		control = { province = 123 data = SOV } #Terijoki (Finland defeated OR gave in)
		OR = { 
			control = { province = 56 data = GER } # Paris is in German hands
			alliance = { country = VIC country = GER }
			event = { id = 2003025 days = 360 } # Baltic recognized as Soviet territory by Germany in Mol-Rib Pact
		} 
		OR = {
			event = { id = 20070280 days = 365 } #at least 1 year after signing (Finland gave in)
			event = { id = 2007036  days = 365 } #at least 1 year after signing (Finland lost)
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = FIN
		event = 20070260 #Demands to Finland
		NOT = { alliance = { country = FIN country = GER } }
		NOT = { alliance = { country = FIN country = ENG } }
		NOT = { event = 2030022 }
		#NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = FIN } }
		OR = { 
			control = { province = 56 data = GER } # Paris is in German hands
			alliance = { country = VIC country = GER }
			event = { id = 2003025 days = 360 } # Baltic recognized as Soviet territory by Germany in Mol-Rib Pact
		} 
		control = { province = 123 data = SOV } #Terijoki (Finland defeated OR gave in)
		NOT = { 
			war = { country = FIN }
			war = { country = GER }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = ROM }
			exists = EST
			exists = LAT
			exists = LIT
		}
		OR = {
			event = { id = 20070280 days = 365 } #at least 1 year after signing (Finland gave in)
			event = { id = 2007036  days = 365 } #at least 1 year after signing (Finland lost)
		}
	}
	
	name = "Annexation of Finland"
	desc = "Following the fall of Paris, and threatening invasion and accusing Finland of violating the original pact as well as forming a conspiracy against the Soviet Union, Moscow presented an ultimatum to Finland, demanding the replacement of its governments and allowing an unlimited number of Soviet troops to enter the country, among other concessions."
	style = 2
	picture = "sov_baltics_end"
	
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1939 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 0 month = december year = 1945 }	

	action = {
		name = "Annex Finland"
		command = { type = trigger which = 3183038 }
	}
}

	country = FIN

Most people have been expecting a rapid and nearly bloodless campaign against a small and sparsely populated neighboring state. They were unpleasantly surprised to find their country entangled in a long and bloody struggle with no apparent end in sight. Even at the Politbureau, people most loyal to Stalin have been advising to find a way to end this war quickly as it was proving to be a dangerous drain on the Soviet treasury and manpower. Ultimately, the weight of the situation and the need to prepare for the war against Germany finally convinced Stalin to offer a compromise peace to Finland which would allow Moscow to save face and claim victory, but end up far short of his original objectives. Essentially, Moscow is demanding that we give up the territory they demanded in their original pre-war proposal of territorial exchange, minus the base at Hanko and without any further political conditions. While it is still not a good deal for Helsinki, rejecting this offer could easily lead us down the primrose path to our total destruction."

event = {
	id = 2007032
	random = no
	country = SOV

	#decision = {
	#	war = { country = GER }
	#	NOT = { ispuppet = GER }
	#	lost_national = { country = SOV value = 5 }
	#}
	#decision_trigger = {
	#}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = { ispuppet = GER }
		random = 35
		OR = {
			AND = {
				control = { province = 56 data = -2 } # Paris is controlled by an enemy
				lost_national = { country = SOV value = 35 }
				NOT = { land_percentage = { country = GER value = 0.3 } } #SOV has not enough troops
				NOT = { lost_national = { country = GER value = 7 } } #Germany is not losing national provinces
				NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } } #Germany is concentrated on the Soviet front
				NOT = {	war = { country = GER country = VIC } }
				NOT = { war = { country = GER country = USA } }
				NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ITA } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { control = { province = 187 data = SOV } } # Leningrad
				NOT = { control = { province = 163 data = SOV } } # Stalingrad
				NOT = { control = { province = 175 data = SOV } } # Moscow
				OR = {
					NOT = { control = { province = 1907 data = SOV } } # Baku
					NOT = { control = { province = 1828 data = SOV } } # Orenburg
				}
				NOT = { lost_national = { country = GER value = 7 } } #Germany is not losing national provinces
				OR = { #one of the following must be true
					local_flag = SOVMoscow  #Stalin remained in Moscow and was captured
					NOT = { control = { province = 1575 data = SOV } } # Sverdlovsk
					NOT = { control = { province = 1372 data = SOV } } # Vladivostok
					AND = { 
						nuked = -1  #was nuked at least once
						NOT = { nuked = 1 } #SOV never launched nukes
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { nuked = 1 } #SOV never launched nukes
				lost_national = { country = SOV value = 15 } #SOV is losing ground
				nuked = { country = GER country = SOV data = 1 where = 175 } # Moscow nuked
				OR = {
					nuked = { country = GER country = SOV data = 8 }#SOV nuked 8 times by GER
					AND = {
						nuked = { country = GER country = SOV data = 1 where = 187 } # Leningrad nuked
						nuked = { country = GER country = SOV data = 1 where = 163 } # Stalingrad nuked
						nuked = { country = GER country = SOV data = 1 where = 1907 } # Baku nuked
					}
				}
			}					
		}
	}
	
	name = "EVT_2624_NAME"
	desc = "EVT_2624_DESC"
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"
	
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 10 # Check for trigger conditions every ten days
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }
	
	action = {
		name = "Propose peace to Germany"
		
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = ENG country = GER }} type = relation which = ENG value = -15 }
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = U01 country = GER }} type = relation which = U01 value = -15 }
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = USA country = GER }} type = relation which = USA value = -15 }
		command = { type = trigger which = 2003077 } # GER
	}
}

	}
	
	name = "Peace Negotiations"
	desc = "After years of brutal and destructive conflict, even the seemingly inexhaustable manpower reserves of the Soviet Union are nearing depletion. Yet, we have been receiving signals from the other side indicating that they are growing tired of this war as well and would prefer to settle it soon, even if their initial goals are nowhere close to achieved. 	}

		war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
		war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		NOT = { war = { country = GER country = JAP } }
	}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
		war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		NOT = { war = { country = GER country = JAP } }
		lost_national = { country = SOV value = 10 }
		lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 10 }
		event = { id = 2007041 days = 365 }
		OR = {
			lost_national = { country = SOV value = 15 }
			lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 15 }
		}
	}
	name = "Xinjiang switches sides"
	desc = "With the USSR distracted by its war with Germany, Sheng approached the Chinese government in July 1942 for reconciliation talks. The final months of 1942 saw the most turbulent period in Xinjiang-Soviet relations. In October 1942 Sheng demanded from the Soviet General Consul that all Soviet technical and military personnel be withdrawn from Xinjiang within three months. Despite Sheng's ultimatum, only in March–April 1943 did the Soviets notify Sheng and the Chinese government of their withdrawal. The Kuomintang representatives and personnel filled the void. In June 1943, four divisions of the NRA New 2nd Army were transferred to Xinjiang from Gansu. In October 1943, the Kuomintang effectively removed Soviet influence from Xinjiang. With the Soviets gone, in September Sheng ordered the arrest and execution of the CCP cadres."
	style = 0

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = "Send ultimatum to the Soviets"
		command = { type = dissent value = 15 } #Communists are very upset

		command = { type = domestic which = political_left value = -1 }
		command = { type = end_trades which = SOV where = SIK }
		command = { type = event which = 203111 where = SOV when = 0 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 150041
	random = no
	country = SOV

	trigger = {
		month = 9
		day = 1
	}
   
	name = "Soviet Forced Industrialisation"
	desc = "Between 1921 and 1926, the Soviet economy rapidly recovered from the chaos of WW1 and Civil War under the program called 'New Economic Policy' (NEP), which combined elements of socialist and capitalist modes of production. By mid 1920s, however, the room for recovery had largely been exhausted, and in 1927, a series of economic crises and a fear of a looming war with the West forced the Soviet leadership to reconsider the continued utility of NEP. There were two competing schools of thought - one argued that the Soviet Union could not afford to slow down its economic growth, especially while surrounded by potentially hostile capitalist powers, and that NEP should be abandoned in favor of a program of rapid forced industrialization by means of nationalisation, collectivisation, and ambitious five-year development goals. Consumer goods spending was to be artificially slashed and capital redirected towards the growth of heavy industry. Any resulting dissent and dissatisfaction would have to be ruthlessly crushed. The other school of thought argued that NEP should be preserved, but the lower rates of growth were natural and expected. They warned that forced industrialization could lead to food shortages, inflation, and peasant uprisings. The debates in the VKP(b) over this subject became really heated, with Joseph Stalin initially backing the supporters of the NEP while Leon Trotsky and his allies advocated for five-year plans. But by 1928, with Trotsky defeated and exiled, Stalin himself had changed his mind on the subject and decided to support forced industrialization as well. In a speech before the 15th Congress of VKP(b), Stalin warned that without an end to economic backwardness 'the advanced countries...will crush us.' Thus, the VKP(b) approved the First Five-Year Plan, which served as the blueprint for future Soviet economic development for many decades."
	style = 2
	
	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1908 }

	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

event = {
	id = 150042
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	tag = { SOV }

	name = "Soviet Forced Industrialisation"
	desc = "The First Five-Year Plan (1928-1933) led to mixed results. Areas like capital goods increased 158\%, consumer goods increased by 87\%, and total industrial output increased by 118\%. Yet, these successes were still far below the goals initially outlined by the planning committee, and the cost to the Soviet people - particularly the peasantry, was very severe. The plan ended with a massive famine of 1932-1933 which claimed the lives of between 3.3 and 7.5 million people, and led to a serious slump in the the overall production, particularly in the agricultural sector. Despite this, the achievements of the first plan laid the foundation for the much more successuful Second Five-Year Plan (1933-1937), which saw an explosive growth in Soviet economic output. While much of the capitalist world was suffering from the Great Depression, the USSR saw overall GNP growth averages of 10\% to 12\% per year. But these achievements also did not come for free, they were only made possible by a forced labor system and an increasingly repressive state apparatus, which led directly to the massive purges of 1937-1938. Finally, the Third Five-Year Plan was adopted in the midst of rising tensions in Europe and focused mostly on the devlopment of war industries and a growth in defense spending. Industry grew at at a pace of 11\% per year and overall GNP growth averaged at about 6\% yearly growth. In 1941, the Third Five-Year plan was abandoned due to the outbreak of the war."

event = {
	id = 2007047
	random = no
	country = SOV

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = GER }
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = USA }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = VIC }
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		policy = { type = professional_army value = 6 }
		OR = {
			lost_national = { value = 15 }
			lost_VP = { value = 20 }
			lost_IC = { value = 15 }
		}
	}

	name = fbarb_event8_name
	desc = fbarb_event8_desc
	style = 2
  	picture = "news_paper"

	date = { day = 1 month = september year = 1937 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }


	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_GREAT" # Great!
		command = { type = dissent value = -10 }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = inMajorWar	}
		command = { type = local_setflag which = popularMobilization }
		
		#GPW-specific bonuses (will last for 5 years with purges, 1 year without purges)
		command = { type = repair_mod 			value = 15 } #reinforcement efficiency
		command = { type = morale which = land 	value = 5  }
		
		#Gearing up 10
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = { which = WAR_level when = 1 value = 10 } } 	NOT = { local_flag = WAR_change } } type = event which = 2030 where = -1 when = 0  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = { which = WAR_level when = 1 value = 10 } }			local_flag = WAR_change   } type = local_setflag which = WAR_manual when = 0 value = 1	}
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = { which = WAR_level when = 1 value = 10 } }			local_flag = WAR_change   } type = event which = 2030 where = -1 when = 0  }
		
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror1 local_flag = SOVterror2 local_flag = SOVterror3 } }	type = event which = 2007050 where = SOV when = 365  }  #War Enthusiasm Wanes - 1 year
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = SOVterror1 local_flag = SOVterror2 local_flag = SOVterror3 }			type = event which = 2007050 where = SOV when = 1825 }	#War Enthusiasm Wanes - 5 years
		
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { technology = 6340 } } type = gain_tech which = 6340 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { technology = 6350 } } type = gain_tech which = 6350 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { technology = 6370 } } type = gain_tech which = 6370 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 2007061
	random = no
	country = SOV

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				war = { country = GER }
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = GER } }
			}
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP }
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = JAP } }
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = MAN } }
			}
			AND = {
				war = { country = ENG }
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = ENG } }
			}
			AND = {
				war = { country = VIC }
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = VIC } }
			}
			AND = {
				war = { country = USA }
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = USA } }
			}
		}
	}

	name = "Opposition to the war"
	desc = "Despite the blatant imperialist aggression, shockingly, there is a part of the Soviet society that is actually blaming Moscow for the escalation and the war. The opposition is heavy across certain spectrums of the Soviet society, but especially among certain non-Russian republics where people have been falsely promised 'liberation' by the enemy. Many are unwilling to fight, even worse, some are willing to fight for the enemy!"
	style = 2

	date = { day = 1 month = october year = 1936 }
	offset = 5
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = october year = 1949 }

	action = {
		name = "We must have not purged enough of them!"
		command = { type = dissent value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { under_attack = SOV } } type = dissent value = 1 }
		
		#+5 dissent if purges never happened (6 total)
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror1 } } type = dissent value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror2 } } type = dissent value = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror3 } } type = dissent value = 3 }
		
		command = { type = event which = 2400151 where = -1 when = 1   } #initial confusion
		
		#More purges - longer period before recovery
		#No purges - recovery in 1 month. Full purges - recovery in 6 months.
		
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = SOVterror1 local_flag = SOVterror2 local_flag = SOVterror3 } 						type = event which = 2400161 where = -1 when = 60 } #recovery in 60 days YYY
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror1 } local_flag = SOVterror2 local_flag = SOVterror3 } 			type = event which = 2400161 where = -1 when = 50 } #recovery in 50 days XYY
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = SOVterror1 NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror2 } local_flag = SOVterror3 } 			type = event which = 2400161 where = -1 when = 50 } #recovery in 50 days YXY
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror1 } NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror2 } local_flag = SOVterror3 }	type = event which = 2400161 where = -1 when = 40 } #recovery in 40 days XXY
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = SOVterror1 local_flag = SOVterror2 NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror3 } } 			type = event which = 2400161 where = -1 when = 30 } #recovery in 30 days YYX
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror1 } local_flag = SOVterror2 NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror3 } } 	type = event which = 2400161 where = -1 when = 20 } #recovery in 20 days XYX
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = SOVterror1 NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror2 } NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror3 } } 	type = event which = 2400161 where = -1 when = 20 } #recovery in 20 days YXX
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { local_flag = SOVterror1 local_flag = SOVterror2 local_flag = SOVterror3 } } 			type = event which = 2400161 where = -1 when = 10 } #recovery in 10 days XXX

	}

##########################################################
# Leningrad forts destroyed as Germans move in
##########################################################
event = {
	id = 23126501
	random = no
	country = SOV

	trigger = {
		NOT = { control = { province = 187 } } #Leningrad
        atwar = yes
	}
	
	name = "Leningrad forts destroyed by combat"
	desc = "Leningrad forts destroyed by combat"
	style = 2

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1999 }

	action = {
		name = "Heavy combat has destroyed the Leningrad forts"
        command = { type = construct which = land_fort where = 187 		value = -5 } #Leningrad
		command = { type = construct which = coastal_fort where = 187	value = -5 } #Leningrad
        command = { type = construct which = flak where = 187 			value = -5 } #Leningrad
	}
}

#####################################################################################
# Moscow surrounded (needed to prevent starvation from capital enriclement)
#####################################################################################
event = { 
	id = 23126530
	random = no
	country = SOV

	trigger= { 
		capital = { province = 175 }
		
		NOT = { 
			control = { province = 176 }
			control = { province = 1155 }
			control = { province = 7082 }
			control = { province = 180 }
			control = { province = 94 }
			control = { province = 7081 }
		}
			
		control = { province = 1830 } # Kuybyshev
	}

	name = "Moscow surrounded - Government evacuates to Kuybyshev"
	desc = "As enemy forces forced closed in on the burning ruins of the Kremlin, the Politburo were already on their way to Kujbyshev. The escape of the government from Moscow was a tremendous blow to the Soviet war effort, and confidence in the Bolshevik leadership was shaken."
	style = 2

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1999 }

	action = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_DAMN
		command = { type = capital which = 1830 } # Kuybyshev
		command = { type = dissent value = 1 }
	}
}

#####################################################################################
# Moscow recaptured
#####################################################################################
event = { 
	id = 23126532
	random = no
	country = SOV

	trigger= { 
		NOT = { capital = { province = 175 } }
		control = { province = 175 }
		control = { province = 176 }
		control = { province = 1155 }
		control = { province = 7082 }
		control = { province = 180 }
		control = { province = 94 }
		control = { province = 7081 }
	}

	name = "Moscow recaptured"
	desc = "With Moscow firmly back in Soviet hands, there was no reason for delaying the return of the Government to what was left of the Kremlin. The recapture of the capital was a huge boost to Soviet morale, and a sign that finally, the tide had turned." 
	style = 2

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1999 }

	action = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_OK
		command = { type = capital which = 175 }
		command = { type = dissent value = -2 }
	}
}
